Term T(a,b)=h_a(1-h_b), a≠b.
If we change a subset S to new values, for each ordered pair (a,b) where a or b in S, delta = T_new - T_old. We can compute for all pairs involving S using vectorization. For S={i,j} values old_i, old_j new_i, new_j (swap), we can zero delta array length m.
For each x in S with old=O, new=N:
  For all y not in S:
    pair (x,y): delta = (N-O)*(1-h_y) if x first. p=x-y+nm1.
    pair (y,x): delta = h_y*(O-N) (since term h_y(1-h_x), second changed, delta = h_y*((1-O)-(1-N))? New-old = h_y(1-N)-h_y(1-O)=h_y(O-N)). p=y-x+nm1.
For pair (i,j) both changed:
  old T(a,b) = Va(1-Vb), new = Vna(1-Vnb), delta = new-old. Add to p=a-b+nm1.
This is O(n) vectorized for each x (2 times slices), plus pair. Need account x,y excluding other modified to avoid double; we exclude S for y, then add pair separately.

def _proj_feasible(v, n):
    v = np.asarray(v,float)
    S = 0.5*n
    # if already sum close and in bounds, return clip?
    lo = np.max(v - np.arange? no)
    # Solve clip(v + lam,0,1) sum=S.
    lo_lam = -v.max() - 2? ub_lam = 1 - v.min() +2
    for 60 bisections:
       mid=(lo+hi)/2
       s=np.sum(np.clip(v+mid,0,1))
       if s < S: lo=mid else hi=mid
    x=np.clip(v+mid,0,1)
    # correction for rounding: adjust freely elements
    d=S-x.sum()
    if abs(d)>1e-12:
       mask=(x>1e-12)&(x<1-1e-12)
       if mask.sum()>0: x[mask]+=d/mask.sum(); clip
       else: x[:] = S/n
    return x
```

Delta function for transfer with di,dj at i,j:
```
def _delta_transfer(h, i, j, di, dj, nm1, info):
    m = 2*len(h)-1
    delta = np.zeros(m)
    if di != 0:
       pf, ps, yl = info[i]
       delta[pf] += di*(1.0 - h[yl])
       delta[ps] -= di*h[yl]
    if dj != 0:
       pf, ps, yl = info[j]
       delta[pf] += dj*(1.0 - h[yl])
       delta[ps] -= dj*h[yl]
    if i != j:
       corr = -di*dj
       if corr != 0:
           delta[i - j + nm1] += corr
           delta[j - i + nm1] += corr
    return delta
```

def _active_lp_local(h,n,rng,max_iter,initial_gap=0.5,max_step=0.05):
   h=h.copy(); c=corr; cur=max(c); gap=initial_gap; no_improve=0
   info=build? For LP need gradients not delta; no info.
   while ...:
      active_idx = np.where(c >= cur - gap)[0]
      if len(active_idx)==0: active_idx=[argmax]
      # maybe choose top if too many: sort active by c descending, take min(len, topk)
      # build grad matrix
      G=[]
      for p in active_idx: G.append(_grad_count(h, p-(n-1)))
      G=np.array(G)
      # Remove rows with norm very small? keep.
      u= max_step*np.minimum? For bounds:
      low = -np.minimum(h, max_step)
      high = -np.minimum(1-h, max_step)? Wait to allow decrease: lb=-min(h_i, max_step); ub=min(1-h_i, max_step). So:
      lb = -np.minimum(h, max_step)
      ub = np.minimum(1-h, max_step)
      bnds = list(zip(lb,ub)); bnds.append((-1e6,1e6))
      A = np.column_stack((G, -np.ones(len(G))))
      b = np.zeros(len(G))
      Aeq = np.zeros((1,n+1)); Aeq[0,:n]=1
      sol=linprog(np.r_[0,1], A_ub=A,b_ub=b,A_eq=Aeq,b_eq=[0],bounds=bnds, method='highs')
      if sol.success and sol.x[n] < -1e-7:
         d=sol.x[:n]
         # line search s in [1,0.5,...]
         improved=False
         for s in [1.0,0.5,0.25,0.1,0.05,0.02]:
             h2 = h + s*d
             if np.all(h2>=-1e-12)&...:
                h2=np.clip(h2,0,1); # sum maybe off; project if needed
                c2=np.correlate(h2,1-h2); val2=max(c2)
                if val2 < cur - 1e-12:
                   accept
         if not improved: fallback
         no_improve=0; cur=val2
      else fallback
```

def _fast_local_search(h, n, max_s, rng, n_iter=200000):
  h = _proj_feasible(h,n).copy(); c=corr; cur=max(c); info=build
  last=recompute; no_improve=0
  for it in range(max_iter):
     if time < max_s-3 break
     # choose move type by rng.random
     if type 0.5 swap, 0.3 small transfer, 0.2 gradient transfer
     ... evaluate up to 3 candidates maybe
     if accepted: update h,c,cur,no_improve=0 else no_improve++
     if no_improve > threshold: break or random restart?
     if it%2000==0: recompute c
  return h, cur*dx

Implementation of _smooth_slsqp:
```
def _smooth_slsqp(h, n, beta, maxiter):
    global _G_N, _G_BETA
    _G_N=n; _G_BETA=float(beta)
    h0=_proj_feasible(h,n)
    bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*n
    try:
       res=scipy_minimize(_smooth_fg, h0, method='SLSQP', jac=True, bounds=bounds, constraints=[{'type':'eq','fun':_eqfun}], options={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-15,'iprint':0})
       return _proj_feasible(res.x,n)
    except: return h0
```

```
def _active_lp_local(h,n,limit_s=60,ignore=0.25):
    h = _proj_feasible(h,n).copy()
    c = np.correlate(h,1-h,'full')
    cur=float(np.max(c))
    best_h=h.copy(); best_c=cur
    step_scale=0.04
    topk=20
    noimp=0
    while _remaining()>limit_s? Actually use while time remaining > min_stop.
       # active choose indices with c >= cur - gap; if < topk use top
       gap = max(0.2, 0.05*cur?) count ~30, 0.05*30=1.5
       active = np.where(c >= cur - gap)[0]
       if len(active) > topk:
           active = active[np.argsort(c[active])[-topk:]]
       G=[]
       for p in active:
          g=_grad_count(h,p-(n-1))
          if norm >1e-12: G.append(g)
       if not G: break
       G=np.array(G)
       lb = -np.minimum(h, step_scale)
       ub = np.minimum(1-h, step_scale)
       # For variables at 0/1, only inward allowed.
       bnds = [(float(lb[i]), float(ub[i])) for i in range(n)] + [(-1e6,1e6)]
       A = np.hstack((G, -np.ones((len(G),1))))
       Aeq = np.zeros((1,n+1)); Aeq[0,:n]=1
       sol=linprog(np.r_[0,n?], A_ub=A,b_ub=np.zeros(len(G)),A_eq=Aeq,b_eq=[0],bounds=bnds,method='highs',options={'presolve':True})
       if sol.success:
          d=sol.x[:n]; mu=sol.x[n]
          if mu < -1e-8:
             improved=False
             for s in [1.0,0.75,0.5,0.25,0.1,0.05]:
                h2 = h + s*d
                if np.all(h2 >= -1e-12) and np.all(h2 <= 1+1e-12):
                   h2 = np.clip(h2,0,1)
                   # enforce sum? projection maybe
                   if abs(h2.sum()-0.5*n)>1e-10:
                       h2=_proj_feasible(h2,n)
                   c2=np.correlate(h2,1-h2); v2=float(np.max(c2))
                   if v2 < cur - 1e-12:
                      h=h2; c=c2; cur=v2; improved=True; break
             if improved:
                noimp=0; if cur<best_c: best=...
                continue
       # fallback steepest on max lag: choose p argmax(c), g
       p=int(np.argmax(c)); g=_grad_count(h,p-(n-1)); gn=norm(g)
       if gn>1e-12:
          d=-g; d-=d.mean()
          # scale to box and step
          scale=step_scale
          # for each i, if d[i]>0: max inc min(1-h_i, step_scale), else dec ...
          # scaling to ensure d*1 within bounds:
          up=ub? We already have ub/lb? Use arrays max_inc, max_dec.
          maxd=0
          for ...?
          # vector: inc = d>0; scale = min(scale? Actually d may large; want step=1* d bounded.
          # Compute s_bound = min( (h_i +? ))
          # if d_i>0: max s = min(1-h_i, step_scale) / d_i? Wait d_i is direction unit? We'll set d = -g normalized? Better scale d by step magnitude.
          # Let's standardize d: d = d / max(abs(d)) then scale max step to fit.
          d= -g; d-=d.mean(); amax=np.max(np.abs(d)); if amax>0: d/=amax
          # fit d within [lb,ub] at s=1:
          fit=1.0
          inc=d>0; dec=d<0
          if np.any(inc): fit=min(fit, np.min(ub[inc]/d[inc]))
          if np.any(dec): fit=min(fit, np.min(ub? For dec, need d_i*s >= lb_i => s >= lb_i/d_i? Wait lb negative, d dec negative; lower bound not constraint for s<1? We require h+s d within [0,1]. For dec, h+s d >=0 => s <= h_i / -d_i. Also h+s d <=1 automatically if d<0. So upper constraint from lower: s <= h[-d]/-d. Use dec: fit=min(fit, np.min(h[dec]/(-d[dec])))

                 ... evaluate

def _build_delta_info(n):
    nm1 = n - 1
    ys = []
    pf = []
    ps = []
    for x in range(n):
        arr = np.arange(n, dtype=np.int64)
        arr = arr[arr != x]
        pf.append(np.arange? x - arr + nm1)
        ps.append(arr - x + nm1)
        ys.append(arr)
    return (ys,pf,ps)
```

`_delta_transfer(h, info, n, i, j, di, dj)` uses info tuple. Need nm1 = n-1. Precompute in info maybe `nm1` to avoid recompute.
```
def _delta_transfer(h, info, n, i, j, di, dj):
    ys, pf, ps = info
    m = 2*n - 1
    delta = np.zeros(m, dtype=float)
    if di != 0.0:
       delta[pf[i]] += di * (1.0 - h[ys[i]])
       delta[ps[i]] -= di * h[ys[i]]
    if dj != 0.0 and j != i:
       delta[pf[j]] += dj * (1.0 - h[ys[j]])
       delta[ps[j]] -= dj * h[ys[j]]
    if i != j:
       corr = -di * dj
       if corr != 0.0:
          nm1 = n - 1
          delta[i - j + nm1] += corr
          delta[j - i + nm1] += corr
    return delta
```
Wait correction should be applied even if di,dj nonzero. If one zero, corr 0. Good.

But if di or dj zero, formula for other x includes y other not excluded; correction zero, exact? If only one variable changes, yes formula linear exact. If both, correction. Good.

Potential problem: If di+dj not zero (sum not preserved), objective still can be evaluated? Constraints require sum n/2. We only use sum-preserving. SLSQP handles. For local search, no projection.

For swap, di = h[j] - h[i], dj = h[i] - h[j]. Then corr = -(h[j]-h[i])(h[i]-h[j]) = (h[j]-h[i])^2 positive. Good.

Let's test delta formula for swap i=0 h1, j=1 h0 in small h=[1,0,0,0]? sum not 0.5? Just test pair. Old p_ij = i-j+nm1. Value 1. New after swap 0, delta -1. di=-1,dj=1, corr=1. Linear included p_ij -2, +corr -1. Good.

def _try_inplace(h, c, info, n, i, j, di, dj, curmax):
    a=h[i]; b=h[j]
    na=a+di; nb=b+dj
    if na < -1e-11 or na > 1+1e-11 or nb ...: return False, 0.0, None
    if i==j or (abs(di)<1e-14 and abs(dj)<1e-14): return False, curmax, None

       c -= delta
       return False, vm, None

Did our delta formula include self? Precomputed yl excludes x, so no self in linear terms. But for variable x, c_{nm1} = ∑ h_i(1-h_i) includes self. Our delta formula omitted self when x changes! def _delta_transfer(...):
   m=2*n-1; nm1=n-1; delta=zeros
   if di:
      delta[pf[i]] += di*(1-h[ys[i]])
      delta[ps[i]] -= di*h[ys[i]]
      delta[nm1] += di*(1-2*h[i]) - di*di
   if dj:
      delta[pf[j]] += dj*(1-h[ys[j]])
      delta[ps[j]] -= dj*h[ys[j]]
      delta[nm1] += dj*(1-2*h[j]) - dj*dj
   if i!=j:
      corr = -di*dj
      delta[i-j+nm1] += corr
      delta[j-i+nm1] += corr
   return delta
```

def _proj_feasible(v,n):
   v=np.asarray(v,float)
   S=0.5*n
   # if v small length? if S==v.sum() and bounds, return
   lo = -v.max() - 2.0
   hi = 1.0 - v.min() + 2.0
   for _ in range(80): ...
   x=np.clip(v+mid,0,1)
   d=S-x.sum()
   if abs(d) > 1e-12:
      mask=(x>1e-10)&(x<1-1e-10)
      k=mask.sum()
      if k>0:
          x[mask] += d/k
          np.clip(x,0,1,out=x)
      else:
          x[:]=S/n
   # one more correction on a free coordinate
   mask=(x>1e-12)&(x<1-1e-12)
   if mask.any():
       idx=np.flatnonzero(mask)[0]
       val=S-np.sum(np.delete(x, idx)? expensive) maybe x.sum() - x[idx]
       new=S - (x.sum() - x[idx])
       if 0<=new<=1: x[idx]=new
   return x
```

Now, fast local search uses `_weighted_grad` which loops active weights. Could be called often; if n=320, weights many, slower. In fast search, we can compute gradient less often and with beta=80 and threshold 1e-6. For n=320, maybe 100 lags * 300? okay.

Let's code `_weighted_grad`:
```
def _weighted_grad(h, c, beta):
    n = len(h)
    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    y = beta * (c - cmax)
    m = float(np.max(y))
    ew = np.exp(y - m)
    sw = float(np.sum(ew))
    if sw <= 0: return np.zeros(n)
    w = ew / sw
    g = np.zeros(n)
    nz = np.where(w > 1e-7)[0]
    nm1 = n - 1
    for p in nz:
        wv = float(w[p])
        if wv > 0:
            g += wv * _grad_count(h, int(p) - nm1)
    return g
```
It doesn't multiply dx; direction okay.

Could use active only if too many: nz sorted? `w > 1e-7` maybe many. For beta=80 and c range 30, exp(-80*3)= -240 ~0, so only top few. Good.

def _fast_local_search(h, n, max_s, rng):
    max_s = min(max_s, _tl())
    if max_s < 1: return h,_c5(h)
    h = _proj_feasible(h,n).copy()
    c = np.correlate(h,1-h,'full')
    cur = float(np.max(c))
    dx = 2.0/n
    best_h = h.copy(); best_c5 = cur*dx
    info = _build_delta_info(n)
    g = None
    grad_counter = 1000 # force
    no_improve = 0
    iterations = 0
    deadline = time.time() + max_s
    alphas = (0.5, 0.25, 0.75, 0.1, 0.9, 0.35, 0.65)
    while time.time() < deadline and _tl() > 2:
       iterations +=1
       if iterations >= grad_counter:
          g = _weighted_grad(h,c,80.0)
          grad_counter = iterations + 200
       r = rng.random_sample()
       accepted = False
       if r < 0.45:
          i = rng.randint(n); j=rng.randint(n)
          while j==i: j=rng.randint(n)
          a=h[i]; b=h[j]; di=b-a
          if abs(di) > 1e-13:
              ok, vm = _try_transfer_inplace(h,c,info,n,i,j,di,a-b,cur)
              if ok: accept...
       elif r < 0.85:
          i,j random; a,b; 
          # choose sign
          if rng.random_sample() <0.5:
             lim = min(1.0-a, b)
             if lim>1e-13:
                t = alphas[rng.randint(len(alphas))] * lim
                ok=... di=t,dj=-t
          else:
             lim = min(1.0-b, a)
             if lim>1e-13:
                t = -alphas[...] * lim
                ok=... di=t,dj=-t
       else:
          if g is None: g=...
          if np.max(g)-np.min(g)>1e-12:
             low_idx = np.argsort(g, kind='stable')
             high_idx = np.argsort(-g, kind='stable')
             # random 2 pairs
             for tries in range(3):
                i=low_idx[rng.randint(min(12,len(low_idx)))]
                j=high_idx[rng.randint(min(12,len(high_idx)))]
                if i==j: continue
                a,b=h[i],h[j]
                # move j->i positive if g_i<g_j
                lim=min(1-a,b)
                if lim>1e-13:
                   t=alphas[rng...]*lim (maybe smaller if g diff small?)
                   ok...
                if ok break
       if accepted:
          no_improve=0
          # maybe update best if vm*dx < best_c5
          if vm*dx < best_c5 - 1e-12:
              best_c5=vm*dx; best_h=h.copy()
       else:
          no_improve +=1
          if no_improve > 30000: break
       if iterations % 2000 == 0:
          c = np.correlate(h,1-h,'full'); cur=float(np.max(c))
          if cur*dx < best_c5: best...
    # final
    h= best_h? Should we continue from best or current? Return best_h exact.
    c5=_c5(best_h)
    if c5 < best_c5: best_c5=c5
    return best_h, best_c5
```

def _gradient_step_candidate(h,n,c,max_step=0.05):
   g=_weighted_grad(h,c,100)
   d=-g; d-=d.mean()
   amax=np.max(np.abs(d)); if amax<=0 return None,None,None
   d = d / amax * max_step
   # Try s in [1,0.5,0.25,0.1,0.05]
   for s in (...):
      h2=h+s*d
      if np.all(h2>=-1e-12) and np.all(h2<=1+1e-12):
         # ensure sum? d zero mean; maybe project if sum error
         if abs(h2.sum()-0.5*n)>1e-10: h2=_proj_feasible(h2,n)
         c2=np.correlate(h2,1-h2); v2=max(c2)
         if v2 < cur - 1e-12: return h2,c2,v2
   return None,None,None
```

Implement `_try_transfer_inplace` returns tuple `(ok, vm)`. It modifies h/c if ok.
```
def _try_transfer_inplace(h,c,info,n,i,j,di,dj,curmax):
    nm1=n-1
    a=h[i]; b=h[j]
    na=a+di; nb=b+dj
    if na < -1e-11 or na > 1.0+1e-11 or nb < -1e-11 or nb > 1.0+1e-11:
        return False, curmax
    if abs(di) < 1e-14 and abs(dj) < 1e-14:
        return False, curmax
    delta = _delta_transfer(h,info,n,i,j,di,dj)
    # If delta all zero? maybe not.
    c += delta
    vm = float(np.max(c))
    if vm < curmax - 1e-13:
        h[i] = na
        h[j] = nb
        # if tiny bounds
        if h[i] < 0.0: h[i]=0.0
        elif h[i] > 1.0: h[i]=1.0
        if h[j] < 0.0: h[j]=0.0
        elif h[j] > 1.0: h[j]=1.0
        return True, vm
    c -= delta
    return False, vm
```
But if clipping changes h after c update, c no longer corresponds. We only clip if tiny overshoot; to be safe, if overshoot >1e-12, reject. Use strict:
```
if na < -1e-12 or na >1+1e-12 ... reject
if na < 0: na=0; elif na>1: na=1; h update; but c may slightly off. Accept if overshoot tiny; error negligible. Or avoid by not allowing values outside by using eps in limits.
```
We'll set bounds check with 1e-12, and after update do `if na < 0: na=0.0` etc. Tiny.

```
def _quick_symmetries(h,n):
    h=_proj_feasible(h,n).copy()
    best=h.copy(); bc=_c5(best)
    # forward shifts
    for s in range(n):
       h2=np.roll(h,s)
       c2=_c5(h2)
       if c2 < bc - 1e-14: best=h2; bc=c2
    hr=best[::-1]
    # evaluate reverse and shifts of reverse
    c2=_c5(hr)
    if c2<bc: best=hr; bc=c2
    for s in range(1,n): # avoid repeat s=0 already
       h2=np.roll(hr,s)
       c2=_c5(h2)
       if c2<bc: best=h2; bc=c2
    return _proj_feasible(best,n), bc
```
But if parent optimized, shifting may take O(n^3) for n=320: 320 correlates -> 32M? Wait each correlate n=320 O102k? 320*100k=32M, okay. For n=500, 500*250k=125M maybe 1s. Fine. Could be 2n shifts, 200 correlates for 160. Good.

But cyclic shift may alter the physical meaning of h over [0,2]; it's just another candidate. Good.

```
def _gen_initial(n, rng):
    best=None; bc=1e100
    t=np.arange(n)
    for period in [4,5,6,7,8,10]:
       for amp in [0.3,0.5,0.8]:
          for ph in [0.0,0.5,1.0,1.5]:
             h=0.5+amp*np.sin(2*np.pi*t/float(period)+ph)
             h=np.clip(h,0,1)
             h=_proj_feasible(h,n)
             c5=_c5(h)
             if c5<bc: best=h; bc=c5
    for _ in range(10):
       h=np.zeros(n)
       k=n//2
       idx=rng.choice(n,k,replace=False)
       h[idx]=1.0
       h=_proj_feasible(h,n)
       c5=_c5(h)
       # maybe add small noise? local later
       if c5<bc: best=h; bc=c5
    # thue-morse
    tm=np.array([(int(i ^ (i>>1)) & 1) for i in range(n)], float) # but no lambda? inside list okay? It's not closure. Could use loop.
```

def _try_direction_full(h,n,c,cur,d):
   # d expected scale already max_step; try s
   for s in [1.0,0.6,0.35,0.2,0.1,0.05]:
      h2 = h + s*d
      if np.any(h2 < -1e-12) or np.any(h2 > 1+1e-12): continue
      if abs(h2.sum()-0.5*n) > 1e-10:
          h2 = _proj_feasible(h2,n)
      c2 = np.correlate(h2,1-h2,'full')
      v2=float(np.max(c2))
      if v2 < cur -1e-12:
         return h2,c2,v2,True
   return None,None,cur,False
```

    mid=0.5*(lo+hi)
    s=np.sum(np.clip(v+mid,0,1))
    if s < S: lo=mid else hi=mid
x=np.clip(v+0.5*(lo+hi),0,1)
```

Let's code `_fast_local_search` in detail:
```
def _fast_local_search(h, n, max_s, rng):
    max_s = min(float(max_s), _tl())
    if max_s <= 0.5:
        h = _proj_feasible(h,n)
        return h, _c5(h)
    h = _proj_feasible(h,n).copy()
    c = np.correlate(h,1.0-h,mode='full')
    cur = float(np.max(c))
    dx = 2.0/n
    best = _proj_feasible(h,n).copy()
    bestc = cur*dx
    info = _build_delta_info(n)
    g = None
    next_grad = 0
    it = 0
    noimp = 0
    alphabetas = (0.5,0.25,0.75,0.1,0.9,0.35,0.65,0.15)
    deadline = time.time() + max_s
    recomp = 1000 if n <= 256 else 2500
    while time.time() < deadline and _tl() > 2.0:
        it += 1
        if it >= next_grad:
            g = _weighted_grad(h,c,80.0)
            next_grad = it + 150
        r = rng.random_sample()
        accepted = False
        if r < 0.45:
            i = int(rng.randint(n)); j = int(rng.randint(n))
            if i != j:
                a=h[i]; b=h[j]
                if abs(a-b) > 1e-13:
                    ok, vm = _try_transfer_inplace(h,c,info,n,i,j,b-a,a-b,cur)
                    if ok: accepted=True; cur=vm
        elif r < 0.85:
            i=...; j=...
            if i != j:
                a=h[i]; b=h[j]
                if rng.random_sample() < 0.5:
                    lim = min(1.0-a, b) - 1e-12
                    if lim > 1e-13:
                        t = alphabetas[int(rng.randint(len(alphabetas)))] * lim
                        ok, vm = _try_transfer_inplace(...,i,j,t,-t,cur)
                        if ok: accepted=True; cur=vm
                else:
                    lim = min(1.0-b, a) - 1e-12
                    if lim > 1e-13:
                        t = -alphabetas[...] * lim
                        ok,... i,j,t,-t
        else:
            if g is None: g=_weighted_grad(h,c,80.0)
            if np.max(g) - np.min(g) > 1e-12 and n > 1:
                low = np.argsort(g)
                high = np.argsort(-g)
                klow = min(12, n)
                khigh = min(12, n)
                for _ in range(3):
                    i = int(low[int(rng.randint(klow))]); j = int(high[int(rng.randint(khigh))])
                    if i != j:
                       a=h[i]; b=h[j]
                       if g[i] < g[j]:
                           lim = min(1.0-a, b) - 1e-12
                           if lim > 1e-13:
                               t = alphabetas[int(rng.randint(len(alphabetas)))] * lim
                               ok,vm = _try... i,j,t,-t
                               if ok: accepted=True; cur=vm; break
                       else:
                           # maybe move i->j
                           lim = min(1.0-b, a) - 1e-12
                           if lim > 1e-13:
                               t = -alphabetas[...] * lim
                               ok,...
        if accepted:
            noimp = 0
        else:
            noimp += 1
            if noimp > 30000:
                break
        if it % recomp == 0:
            c = np.correlate(h,1.0-h,mode='full')
            cur = float(np.max(c))
            ev = cur*dx
            if ev < bestc - 1e-14:
                bestc = ev; best = h.copy()
    # final exact evaluate current and maybe best
    ev = _c5(h)
    if ev < bestc: bestc=ev; best=h.copy()
    else: best=_proj_feasible(best,n); bestc=_c5(best) # in case drift
    return best, bestc
```
Note if `best` was h.copy at start; `h` mutated. If best remains start, h current maybe worse; return start. Good.

```
def _active_lp_local(h,n,max_s):
  max_s=min(max_s,_tl()); if max_s<3 return h,_c5(h)
  h=_proj_feasible(h,n).copy(); c=corr; cur=max; best...; info? no.
  noimp=0; deadline=time.time()+max_s
  step=0.04
  topk=25
  while time<deadline and _tl()>2:
     # choose active
     ordered=np.argsort(c)[::-1]
     gap=max(0.3, 0.1*min(5.0, cur?)) # if cur count 30 ->3
     active=[]
     for p in ordered:
        if c[p] >= cur - gap: active.append(int(p))
        if len(active)>=topk: break
     if len(active)<2: active=ordered[:3].tolist()
     Glist=[]
     nm1=n-1
     for p in active:
        g=_grad_count(h, int(p)-nm1)
        gn=float(np.linalg.norm(g))
        if gn>1e-10: Glist.append(g)
     did=False
     if Glist:
        G=np.vstack(Glist)
        lb = -np.minimum(h, step)
        ub = np.minimum(1.0 - h, step)
        mact=len(G)
        A=np.empty((mact,n+1))
        A[:,:n]=G; A[:,n]=-1.0
        Aeq=np.zeros((1,n+1)); Aeq[0,:n]=1.0
        bounds=[(float(lb[i]),float(ub[i])) for i in range(n)]
        bounds.append((-1e9,1e9))
        obj=np.zeros(n+1); obj[n]=1.0
        try:
           sol=linprog(obj, A_ub=A, b_ub=np.zeros(mact), A_eq=Aeq, b_eq=[0.0], bounds=bounds, method='highs', options={'presolve': True})
        except Exception:
           sol=None
        if sol is not None and sol.success:
           d=sol.x[:n]
           mu=float(sol.x[n])
           if mu < -1e-9:
              # ensure d finite and nonzero
              if np.all(np.isfinite(d)) and float(np.max(np.abs(d))) > 1e-12:
                 h2,c2,v2,ok = _try_direction_full(h,n,c,cur,d)
                 if ok: did=True; h=h2; c=c2; cur=v2
     if did:
        noimp=0
        exact=cur*dx; if exact<bestc: ...
        continue
     # fallback gradient
     h2,c2,v2,ok = _gradient_step_candidate(h,n,c,cur,step)
     if ok:
        did=True; h=h2; c=c2; cur=v2
     if not did:
        noimp += 1
        if noimp > 40: break
  # final exact
```
In `_try_direction_full`, if d from LP may not have zero sum due linprog tolerance; it projects if sum error. Good. If d huge? bounds step. Good.

`_gradient_step_candidate`:
```
def _gradient_step_candidate(h,n,c,cur,max_step):
   g=_weighted_grad(h,c,100.0)
   d=-g
   d -= float(np.mean(d))
   amax=float(np.max(np.abs(d)))
   if amax<1e-14: return None,None,cur,False
   d = d / amax * max_step
   # fit s feasible:
   smax=1.0
   inc = d > 1e-14
   if np.any(inc):
       vals = (1.0 - h[inc]) / d[inc]
       vals = vals[vals >= 0]
       if len(vals): smax=min(smax,float(np.min(vals)))
   dec = d < -1e-14
   if np.any(dec):
       vals = h[dec] / (-d[dec])
       if len(vals): smax=min(smax,float(np.min(vals)))
   if smax <= 1e-12: return None,None,cur,False
   d *= smax
   # try full
   for s in [1.0,0.6,0.35,0.2,0.1,0.05]:
       h2 = h + s*d
       if np.min(h2) >= -1e-12 and np.max(h2) <= 1+1e-12:
          if abs(h2.sum()-0.5*n) > 1e-10:
              h2 = _proj_feasible(h2,n)
          c2=np.correlate(h2,1-h2,'full')
          v2=float(np.max(c2))
          if v2 < cur - 1e-12:
              return h2,c2,v2,True
   return None,None,cur,False
```
`vals` could divide by zero; inc ensures d positive. Some vals negative if h>1? no. Good.

Now, `_try_direction_full` used for LP with d already fitted. It can use `_proj_feasible` if out slightly.
```
def _try_direction_full(h,n,c,cur,d):
   for s in [1.0,0.6,0.35,0.2,0.1,0.05]:
      h2=h+s*d
      if np.min(h2) >= -1e-10 and np.max(h2) <= 1+1e-10:
         if abs(h2.sum()-0.5*n)>1e-10: h2=_proj_feasible(h2,n)
         c2=corr; v2=max
         if v2<cur-1e-12: return h2,c2,v2,True
   return None,None,cur,False
```
Need pass cur.

```
import numpy as np
import time
import math
from scipy.optimize import minimize, linprog

_T0 = 0.0
_TB = 1300.0
_G_N = 160
_G_BETA = 200.0

_tl:
```
def _tl():
    return max(0.0, _TB - (time.time() - _T0) - 3.0)
```

_c5:
```
def _c5(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=np.float64)
    n = len(h)
    if n == 0: return 1e100
    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
    return float(np.max(c)) * (2.0 / n)
```

_proj_feasible:
```
def _proj_feasible(v, n):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    S = 0.5 * n
    if v.size != n:
        # if wrong length? pad/truncate? Not expected
    lo = -float(np.max(v)) - 2.0
    hi = 1.0 - float(np.min(v)) + 2.0
    if hi < lo: ... no
    for _ in range(70):
        mid = 0.5*(lo+hi)
        s = float(np.sum(np.clip(v + mid, 0.0, 1.0)))
        if s < S:
            lo = mid
        else:
            hi = mid
    x = np.clip(v + 0.5*(lo+hi), 0.0, 1.0)
    d = S - float(np.sum(x))
    if abs(d) > 1e-13:
        mask = (x > 1e-10) & (x < 1.0 - 1e-10)
        k = int(np.sum(mask))
        if k > 0:
            x[mask] += d / k
            np.clip(x, 0.0, 1.0, out=x)
        else:
            x[:] = S / n
    mask = (x > 1e-12) & (x < 1.0 - 1e-12)
    if np.any(mask):
        idxs = np.flatnonzero(mask)
        idx = int(idxs[0])
        newval = S - (float(np.sum(x)) - float(x[idx]))
        if 0.0 <= newval <= 1.0:
            x[idx] = newval
    return x
```
If n=0? not.

_interp_f:
```
def _interp_f(h, ns, nd):
    h=np.asarray(h,float)
    if ns == nd: return h.copy()
    xs=(np.arange(ns)+0.5)*(2.0/ns)
    xd=(np.arange(nd)+0.5)*(2.0/nd)
    return np.interp(xd, xs, h)
```

_grad_count:
```
def _grad_count(h, k):
    n=len(h)
    g=np.zeros(n)
    if k >= 0:
        if k == 0:
            g[:] = 1.0 - 2.0*h
        else:
            a=0; b=n-k
            g[k:] += 1.0 - h[a:b]
            g[a:b] -= h[k:]
    else:
        d=-k
        a=0; b=n-d
        g[a:b] += 1.0 - h[a+d:b+d]
        g[a+d:b+d] -= h[a:b]
    return g
```
Check for k positive with k>0, `g[k:]` length n-k? In numpy, g[k:] length n-k, yes. h[a:b]=h[0:n-k]. Good. For k=0, use direct to avoid `g[0:]` etc also works but okay. For k negative, b=n-d. `h[a+d:b+d]` = h[d:n-d+d]? d + (n-d) = n, okay. Good.

_smooth_fg:
```
def _smooth_fg(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=np.float64)
    n = _G_N
    beta = float(_G_BETA)
    dx = 2.0 / n
    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    y = beta * (c - cmax)
    m = float(np.max(y))
    ew = np.exp(y - m)
    sw = float(np.sum(ew))
    if sw <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(sw):
        return cmax * dx, np.zeros(n)
    w = ew / sw
    g = np.zeros(n)
    nz = np.where(w > 1e-8)[0]
    nm1 = n - 1
    for p in nz:
        wv = float(w[int(p)])
        if wv > 0.0:
            g += wv * _grad_count(h, int(p) - nm1)
    val = cmax + (m + math.log(sw)) / beta
    return float(val * dx), g * dx
```
Since m should be 0. If beta huge, y-m maybe, okay.

_eq_fun:
```
def _eq_fun(h):
    return float(np.sum(h)) - 0.5 * _G_N
```

_smooth_slsqp:
```
def _smooth_slsqp(h, n, beta, max_s=60.0):
    h = _proj_feasible(h, n).copy()
    max_s = min(float(max_s), _tl())
    if max_s < 4.0:
        return h
    global _G_N, _G_BETA
    _G_N = int(n)
    _G_BETA = float(beta)
    maxiter = int(max(40, min(240, 120.0 * min(1.0, max_s / 30.0) * (160.0 / float(n)))))
    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n
    try:
        res = minimize(_smooth_fg, h, method='SLSQP', jac=True,
                       bounds=bounds,
                       constraints=[{'type': 'eq', 'fun': _eq_fun}],
                       options={'maxiter': maxiter, 'ftol': 1e-14, 'iprint': 0})
        x = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64) if hasattr(res, 'x') else h
    except Exception:
        x = h
    return _proj_feasible(x, n)
```
If max_s=250, factor min(1,8.3)=1, n=160 ->120. Good. If max_s=300, max 120. Maybe too low for high beta; but okay. Could cap 200. Set min(260, 160...). Use 160. For final high maybe 200.

```

_weighted_grad:
```
def _weighted_grad(h, c, beta):
    n=len(h)
    cmax=float(np.max(c))
    y=beta*(c-cmax)
    m=float(np.max(y))
    ew=np.exp(y-m)
    sw=float(np.sum(ew))
    if sw <=0 or not finite: zeros
    w=ew/sw
    g=zeros
    nm1=n-1
    nz=np.where(w>1e-7)[0]
    for p in nz:
       wv=float(w[p])
       if wv>0: g += wv*_grad_count(h,int(p)-nm1)
    return g
```

_gradient_step_candidate as above. Need `np.isfinite`.

_build_delta_info:
```
def _build_delta_info(n):
    allidx = np.arange(n, dtype=np.int64)
    ys=[]; pf=[]; ps=[]
    for x in range(n):
        mask = np.ones(n, dtype=bool)
        mask[x] = False
        arr = allidx[mask]
        pf.append((int(x) - arr + (n - 1)).astype(np.int64))
        ps.append((arr - int(x) + (n - 1)).astype(np.int64))
        ys.append(arr)
    return (ys, pf, ps)
```
This alloc mask each x. Good.

_delta_transfer:
```
def _delta_transfer(h, info, n, i, j, di, dj):
    ys, pf, ps = info
    m = 2*n - 1
    delta = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
    nm1 = n - 1
    if di != 0.0:
        yi = ys[i]
        delta[pf[i]] += di * (1.0 - h[yi])
        delta[ps[i]] -= di * h[yi]
        delta[nm1] += di * (1.0 - 2.0*h[i]) - di*di
    if dj != 0.0 and j != i:
        yj = ys[j]
        delta[pf[j]] += dj * (1.0 - h[yj])
        delta[ps[j]] -= dj * h[yj]
        delta[nm1] += dj * (1.0 - 2.0*h[j]) - dj*dj
    if i != j:
        corr = -di * dj
        if corr != 0.0:
            delta[i - j + nm1] += corr
            delta[j - i + nm1] += corr
    return delta
```
If i==j? not used. If dj=0 but i!=j, cross corr 0; self j no. Good.

_try_transfer_inplace:
```
def _try_transfer_inplace(h, c, info, n, i, j, di, dj, curmax):
    if i == j:
        return False, curmax
    a = h[i]; b = h[j]
    na = a + di; nb = b + dj
    if na < -1e-12 or na > 1.0 + 1e-12 or nb < -1e-12 or nb > 1.0 + 1e-12:
        return False, curmax
    if abs(di) < 1e-15 and abs(dj) < 1e-15:
        return False, curmax
    delta = _delta_transfer(h, info, n, i, j, di, dj)
    c += delta
    vm = float(np.max(c))
    if vm < curmax - 1e-13:
        if na < 0.0: na=0.0
        elif na > 1.0: na=1.0
        if nb < 0.0: nb=0.0
        elif nb > 1.0: nb=1.0
        h[i] = na
        h[j] = nb
        return True, vm
    c -= delta
    return False, vm
```
If c drift makes vm lower? okay.

_try_direction_full:
```
def _try_direction_full(h, n, c, cur, d):
    d = np.asarray(d, dtype=np.float64)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(d)): return None,None,cur,False
    slist = (1.0, 0.6, 0.35, 0.2, 0.1, 0.05)
    S = 0.5*n
    for s in slist:
        h2 = h + s*d
        minv=float(np.min(h2)); maxv=float(np.max(h2))
        if minv < -1e-10 or maxv > 1.0+1e-10:
            continue
        if abs(float(np.sum(h2)) - S) > 1e-10:
            h2 = _proj_feasible(h2, n)
        else:
            h2 = np.clip(h2,0.0,1.0)
        c2 = np.correlate(h2, 1.0 - h2, mode='full')
        v2 = float(np.max(c2))
        if v2 < cur - 1e-12:
            return h2, c2, v2, True
    return None,None,cur,False
```
If min slightly negative but within 1e-10, clip.

_gradient_step_candidate:
```
def _gradient_step_candidate(h, n, c, cur, max_step):
    g = _weighted_grad(h, c, 100.0)
    d = -g
    d -= float(np.mean(d))
    amax = float(np.max(np.abs(d)))
    if amax < 1e-14:
        return None,None,cur,False
    d = (d / amax) * max_step
    smax = 1.0
    inc = d > 1e-14
    if np.any(inc):
        vals = (1.0 - h[inc]) / d[inc]
        vals = vals[vals > 0.0]
        if vals.size:
            smax = min(smax, float(np.min(vals)))
    dec = d < -1e-14
    if np.any(dec):
        vals = h[dec] / (-d[dec])
        vals = vals[vals > 0.0]
        if vals.size:
            smax = min(smax, float(np.min(vals)))
    if smax > 1.0:
        smax = 1.0
    if smax < 1e-12:
        return None,None,cur,False
    d = d * smax
    return _try_direction_full(h, n, c, cur, d)
```
If some h near bound and d inc, vals could be zero if h=1; smax zero -> fail. Good.

_active_lp_local:
```
def _active_lp_local(h, n, max_s):
    max_s = min(float(max_s), _tl())
    if max_s < 3.0:
        return _proj_feasible(h,n), _c5(h)
    h = _proj_feasible(h,n).copy()
    c = np.correlate(h,1.0-h,mode='full')
    cur = float(np.max(c))
    dx = 2.0/n
    best = h.copy(); bestc = cur*dx
    noimp = 0
    deadline = time.time() + max_s
    step = 0.04
    topk = 25
    while time.time() < deadline and _tl() > 2.0:
        ordered = np.argsort(c)[::-1]
        gap = max(0.3, 0.12 * float(c[int(ordered[0])]))? cur maybe. Use gap = max(0.3, 0.1*cur)
        active=[]
        for p in ordered:
            pp=int(p)
            if float(c[pp]) >= cur - gap:
                active.append(pp)
            if len(active) >= topk:
                break
        if len(active) < 2:
            for p in ordered[:3]: active.append(int(p))
        Glist=[]
        nm1=n-1
        for p in active:
            gg = _grad_count(h, p - nm1)
            if float(np.linalg.norm(gg)) > 1e-10:
                Glist.append(gg)
        improved = False
        if Glist:
            G = np.vstack(Glist)
            lb = -np.minimum(h, step)
            ub = np.minimum(1.0 - h, step)
            mact = len(G)
            A = np.empty((mact, n+1), dtype=np.float64)
            A[:, :n] = G
            A[:, n] = -1.0
            Aeq = np.zeros((1, n+1), dtype=np.float64)
            Aeq[0, :n] = 1.0
            bounds = []
            for i in range(n):
                bounds.append((float(lb[i]), float(ub[i])))
            bounds.append((-1e9, 1e9))
            obj = np.zeros(n+1)
            obj[n] = 1.0
            sol = None
            try:
                sol = linprog(obj, A_ub=A, b_ub=np.zeros(mact), A_eq=Aeq, b_eq=[0.0],
                              bounds=bounds, method='highs',
                              options={'presolve': True})
            except Exception:
                sol = None
            if sol is not None and getattr(sol, 'success', False):
                d = np.asarray(sol.x[:n], dtype=np.float64)
                mu = float(sol.x[n])
                if mu < -1e-9:
                    if np.all(np.isfinite(d)) and float(np.max(np.abs(d))) > 1e-13:
                        h2, c2, v2, ok = _try_direction_full(h, n, c, cur, d)
                        if ok:
                            h = h2; c = c2; cur = v2
                            improved = True
        if not improved:
            h2, c2, v2, ok = _gradient_step_candidate(h, n, c, cur, step)
            if ok:
                h = h2; c = c2; cur = v2
                improved = True
        if improved:
            noimp = 0
            ev = cur * dx
            if ev < bestc - 1e-14:
                bestc = ev; best = h.copy()
        else:
            noimp += 1
            if noimp > 40:
                break
    ev = _c5(h)
    if ev < bestc:
        best = h.copy(); bestc = ev
    else:
        best = _proj_feasible(best, n); bestc = _c5(best)
    return best, bestc
```
Potential: `np.vstack(Glist)` if Glist list of arrays; okay. If topk active grows 3 with duplicates if len<2 from ordered may have duplicates? ordered unique, fine.

Note: In LP, active selected top until gap stop; if gap large, active top topk. Good.

```
def _quick_symmetries(h,n):
    h = _proj_feasible(h,n).copy()
    best = h.copy(); bc = _c5(best)
    max_shifts = n
    if n > 600: max_shifts = 120
    for s in range(max_shifts):
       h2=np.roll(h,s)
       c2=_c5(h2)
       if c2 < bc - 1e-14:
          best=h2 copy? np.roll returns new; best=h2 maybe, but h2 could be view? np.roll copy. bc=c2
    # reverse of original best? Use best current.
    hr = best[::-1].copy()
    c2 = _c5(hr)
    if c2 < bc: best=hr; bc=c2
    for s in range(1,max_shifts):
       h2=np.roll(hr,s)
       c2=_c5(h2)
       if c2<bc: best=h2; bc=c2
    return _proj_feasible(best,n), float(bc)
```
If we do shifts of `best` after first phase, then reverse; okay.

Now, `_gen_initial`:
```
def _gen_initial(n, rng):
    best = None; bc = 1e100
    t = np.arange(n, dtype=float)
    periods = [3,4,5,6,7,8,10,12]
    amps = [0.2,0.35,0.5,0.7]
    phases = [0.0,0.5,1.0,1.5,2.0,2.5]
    per = 0
    for period in periods:
       for amp in amps:
          for ph in phases:
             h = 0.5 + amp * np.sin(2.0*np.pi*t/float(period) + ph)
             h = np.clip(h,0,1)
             h = _proj_feasible(h,n)
             c5 = _c5(h)
             if c5 < bc: bc=c5; best=h.copy()
    # random binary
    k = n//2
    for _ in range(20):
       h=np.zeros(n)
       idx = rng.choice(n, k, replace=False)
       h[idx]=1.0
       h = _proj_feasible(h,n)
       c5 = _c5(h)
       if c5 < bc:
          bc=c5; best=h.copy()
    # Thue-Morse
    h=np.zeros(n)
    for i in range(n):
       x = i
       # bit count of i xor i>>1
       y = i ^ (i >> 1)
       bcprm = y.bit_count() if hasattr(int,'bit_count')? Python 3.8? bit_count introduced 3.8? maybe. Use bin count? `bin(y).count('1')` okay.
       if bcprm & 1:
          h[i]=1.0
    # adjust sum? Thue length 128 has 64, 160 maybe 78? project handles.
    h = _proj_feasible(h,n)
    c5 = _c5(h)
    if c5 < bc: bc=c5; best=h.copy()
    # add small noise to best
    if best is None: best = np.full(n,0.5)
    return _proj_feasible(best,n)
```
`int.bit_count` may exist; for compatibility use simple loop or `bin(y).count("1")`. Fine 20* n. Good.

_pair_sweep:
```
def _pair_sweep(h, n, max_s):
    max_s = min(float(max_s), _tl())
    if max_s < 2.0:
       return _proj_feasible(h,n), _c5(h)
    h = _proj_feasible(h,n).copy()
    c = np.correlate(h,1.0-h,mode='full')
    cur = float(np.max(c)); dx=2.0/n
    best=h.copy(); bestc=cur*dx
    info=_build_delta_info(n)
    noimp=0; pairs=0
    deadline=time.time()+max_s
    recomp=500 if n<=200 else 1200
    alphas=(0.5,0.2)
    for i in range(n):
       if time.time() >= deadline or _tl() <=2: break
       hi=h[i]
       for j in range(i+1,n):
          if time.time() >= deadline or _tl() <=2: break
          pairs +=1
          hj=h[j]
          a=hi; b=hj
          accepted=False
          if abs(a-b) > 1e-13:
             ok,vm = _try_transfer_inplace(h,c,info,n,i,j,b-a,a-b,cur)
             if ok:
                cur=vm; hi=h[i]; hj=h[j]; accepted=True
          if not accepted:
             # positive i gets t
             lim = min(1.0 - a, b) - 1e-12
             if lim > 1e-13:
                for al in (0.5,0.2,0.8):
                   t = al * lim
                   ok,vm = _try_transfer_inplace(h,c,info,n,i,j,t,-t,cur)
                   if ok:
                      cur=vm; hi=h[i]; hj=h[j]; accepted=True
                      break
          if not accepted:
             lim = min(1.0 - b, a) - 1e-12
             if lim > 1e-13:
                for al in (0.5,0.2,0.8):
                   t = -al * lim
                   ok,vm = _try_transfer_inplace(h,c,info,n,i,j,t,-t,cur)
                   if ok:
                      cur=vm; hi=h[i]; hj=h[j]; accepted=True
                      break
          if accepted:
             noimp=0
             # update current a,b for later? hi/hj updated; hi variable maybe; for next j, hi=h[i]; but h[i] may change. Set hi = h[i] after accept? If accept changed j, h[i] maybe changed too. We set hi=h[i].
             hi = h[i]
          else:
             noimp += 1
          if pairs % recomp == 0:
             c = np.correlate(h,1.0-h,mode='full')
             cur = float(np.max(c))
             ev = cur*dx
             if ev < bestc - 1e-14:
                bestc=ev; best=h.copy()
             hi = h[i]
             # if noimp huge? break maybe
             if noimp > 5000:
                noimp=0
                # don't break, continue; recompute
    ev = _c5(h)
    if ev < bestc: best=h.copy(); bestc=ev
    else:
       best=_proj_feasible(best,n); bestc=_c5(best)
    return best,bestc
```
Potential bug: In inner loop, `hi` may be stale if h[i] changed by a move with previous j. We set hi=h[i] after recompute and after accept. But if previous j accepted changed i, hi updated. Good. If previous j not accepted but h unchanged. `hj` not used except b = hj? Actually b = h[j] maybe stale if h[j] changed earlier in loop? j unique increasing; h[j] could have changed as i in previous pairs or in same i? For pair (i,j), h[j] may have changed when processing pair (j, something) in future? Not yet. It could have changed when processing pair (p,i) with p<j? If h[i]? For h[j], only previous p<j paired with j, yes if p from 0 to i-1, out loops? Outer i increments; for current i, previous pairs p<i included j if j>p, so h[j] may have changed. We assign `hj = h[j]` at start of inner, good. For `a=hi`, use current h[i]; hi updated after accepts and recompute. Good.

But after an accepted swap between i and j, values at i and j swapped. For subsequent j, hi is h[i] updated. For current j, no more. Good.

If `_try_transfer_inplace` accepts a small transfer, c drift. Recompute every 500 pairs. Good.

Need if n small 1? inner loops none.

Potential performance: Recompute c full O(n^2) every 500 pairs; for n=160, 12.8k pairs ->25 recomputes, okay.

```python
import numpy as np
import time
import math
from scipy.optimize import minimize, linprog

_T0 = 0.0
_TB = 1300.0
_G_N = 160
_G_BETA = 200.0


def _tl():
    return max(0.0, _TB - (time.time() - _T0) - 3.0)


def _c5(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=np.float64)
    n = len(h)
    if n == 0:
        return 1e100
    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
    return float(np.max(c)) * (2.0 / n)


def _proj_feasible(v, n):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    if v.size != n:
        tmp = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        m = min(n, v.size)
        if m > 0:
            tmp[:m] = v[:m]
        v = tmp
    S = 0.5 * n
    if n == 0:
        return v
    lo = -float(np.max(v)) - 2.0
    hi = 1.0 - float(np.min(v)) + 2.0
    for _ in range(70):
        mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        s = float(np.sum(np.clip(v + mid, 0.0, 1.0)))
        if s < S:
            lo = mid
        else:
            hi = mid
    x = np.clip(v + 0.5 * (lo + hi), 0.0, 1.0)
    d = S - float(np.sum(x))
    if abs(d) > 1e-13:
        mask = (x > 1e-10) & (x < 1.0 - 1e-10)
        k = int(np.sum(mask))
        if k > 0:
            x[mask] += d / k
            np.clip(x, 0.0, 1.0, out=x)
        else:
            x[:] = S / n
    mask = (x > 1e-12) & (x < 1.0 - 1e-12)
    if np.any(mask):
        idxs = np.flatnonzero(mask)
        idx = int(idxs[0])
        total = float(np.sum(x))
        newval = S - (total - float(x[idx]))
        if 0.0 <= newval <= 1.0:
            x[idx] = newval
    return x


def _interp_f(h, ns, nd):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=np.float64)
    if ns == nd:
        return h.copy()
    xs = (np.arange(ns) + 0.5) * (2.0 / ns)
    xd = (np.arange(nd) + 0.5) * (2.0 / nd)
    return np.interp(xd, xs, h)


def _grad_count(h, k):
    n = len(h)
    g = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if k >= 0:
        if k == 0:
            g[:] = 1.0 - 2.0 * h
        else:
            b = n - k
            g[k:] += 1.0 - h[0:b]
            g[0:b] -= h[k:]
    else:
        d = -k
        b = n - d
        g[0:b] += 1.0 - h[d:n]
        g[d:n] -= h[0:b]
    return g


def _smooth_fg(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(_G_N)
    beta = float(_G_BETA)
    dx = 2.0 / n
    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    y = beta * (c - cmax)
    m = float(np.max(y))
    ew = np.exp(y - m)
    sw = float(np.sum(ew))
    if sw <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(sw):
        return cmax * dx, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    w = ew / sw
    g = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    nz = np.where(w > 1e-8)[0]
    nm1 = n - 1
    for p in nz:
        wv = float(w[int(p)])
        if wv > 0.0:
            g += wv * _grad_count(h, int(p) - nm1)
    val = cmax + (m + math.log(sw)) / beta
    return float(val * dx), g * dx


def _eq_fun(h):
    return float(np.sum(h)) - 0.5 * _G_N


def _smooth_slsqp(h, n, beta, max_s=60.0):
    h = _proj_feasible(h, int(n)).copy()
    n = int(n)
    max_s = min(float(max_s), _tl())
    if max_s < 12.0:
        return h
    global _G_N, _G_BETA
    _G_N = n
    _G_BETA = float(beta)
    fac = min(1.0, max_s / 45.0)
    maxiter = int(max(30, min(260, 170.0 * fac * (160.0 / float(n)))))
    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n
    x = h
    try:
        res = minimize(_smooth_fg, h, method='SLSQP', jac=True,
                       bounds=bounds,
                       constraints=[{'type': 'eq', 'fun': _eq_fun}],
                       options={'maxiter': maxiter, 'ftol': 1e-14, 'iprint': 0})
        if hasattr(res, 'x'):
            x = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
    except Exception:
        x = h
    return _proj_feasible(x, n)


def _weighted_grad(h, c, beta):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=np.float64)
    n = len(h)
    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    y = beta * (c - cmax)
    m = float(np.max(y))
    ew = np.exp(y - m)
    sw = float(np.sum(ew))
    if sw <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(sw):
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    w = ew / sw
    g = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    nz = np.where(w > 1e-7)[0]
    nm1 = n - 1
    for p in nz:
        wv = float(w[int(p)])
        if wv > 0.0:
            g += wv * _grad_count(h, int(p) - nm1)
    return g


def _build_delta_info(n):
    n = int(n)
    allidx = np.arange(n, dtype=np.int64)
    ys = []
    pf = []
    ps = []
    nm1 = n - 1
    for x in range(n):
        mask = np.ones(n, dtype=bool)
        mask[x] = False
        arr = allidx[mask]
        pf.append((int(x) - arr + nm1).astype(np.int64))
        ps.append((arr - int(x) + nm1).astype(np.int64))
        ys.append(arr)
    return (ys, pf, ps)


def _delta_transfer(h, info, n, i, j, di, dj):
    n = int(n)
    ys, pf, ps = info
    m = 2 * n - 1
    nm1 = n - 1
    delta = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
    if di != 0.0:
        yi = ys[i]
        delta[pf[i]] += di * (1.0 - h[yi])
        delta[ps[i]] -= di * h[yi]
        delta[nm1] += di * (1.0 - 2.0 * h[i]) - di * di
    if dj != 0.0 and j != i:
        yj = ys[j]
        delta[pf[j]] += dj * (1.0 - h[yj])
        delta[ps[j]] -= dj * h[yj]
        delta[nm1] += dj * (1.0 - 2.0 * h[j]) - dj * dj
    if i != j:
        corr = -di * dj
        if corr != 0.0:
            delta[i - j + nm1] += corr
            delta[j - i + nm1] += corr
    return delta


def _try_transfer_inplace(h, c, info, n, i, j, di, dj, curmax):
    n = int(n)
    if i == j:
        return False, curmax
    a = float(h[i])
    b = float(h[j])
    na = a + di
    nb = b + dj
    if na < -1e-12 or na > 1.0 + 1e-12 or nb < -1e-12 or nb > 1.0 + 1e-12:
        return False, curmax
    if abs(di) < 1e-15 and abs(dj) < 1e-15:
        return False, curmax
    delta = _delta_transfer(h, info, n, i, j, di, dj)
    c += delta
    vm = float(np.max(c))
    if vm < curmax - 1e-13:
        if na < 0.0:
            na = 0.0
        elif na > 1.0:
            na = 1.0
        if nb < 0.0:
            nb = 0.0
        elif nb > 1.0:
            nb = 1.0
        h[i] = na
        h[j] = nb
        return True, vm
    c -= delta
    return False, vm


def _try_direction_full(h, n, c, cur, d):
    n = int(n)
    d = np.asarray(d, dtype=np.float64)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(d)):
        return None, None, cur, False
    slist = (1.0, 0.6, 0.35, 0.2, 0.1, 0.05)
    S = 0.5 * float(n)
    for s in slist:
        h2 = h + s * d
        minv = float(np.min(h2))
        maxv = float(np.max(h2))
        if minv < -1e-10 or maxv > 1.0 + 1e-10:
            continue
        if abs(float(np.sum(h2)) - S) > 1e-10:
            h2 = _proj_feasible(h2, n)
        else:
            h2 = np.clip(h2, 0.0, 1.0)
        c2 = np.correlate(h2, 1.0 - h2, mode='full')
        v2 = float(np.max(c2))
        if v2 < cur - 1e-12:
            return h2, c2, v2, True
    return None, None, cur, False


def _gradient_step_candidate(h, n, c, cur, max_step):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(n)
    g = _weighted_grad(h, c, 100.0)
    d = -g
    d -= float(np.mean(d))
    amax = float(np.max(np.abs(d)))
    if amax < 1e-14:
        return None, None, cur, False
    d = (d / amax) * max_step
    smax = 1.0
    inc = d > 1e-14
    if np.any(inc):
        vals = (1.0 - h[inc]) / d[inc]
        vals = vals[vals > 0.0]
        if vals.size:
            smax = min(smax, float(np.min(vals)))
    dec = d < -1e-14
    if np.any(dec):
        vals = h[dec] / (-d[dec])
        vals = vals[vals > 0.0]
        if vals.size:
            smax = min(smax, float(np.min(vals)))
    if smax > 1.0:
        smax = 1.0
    if smax < 1e-12:
        return None, None, cur, False
    d = d * smax
    return _try_direction_full(h, n, c, cur, d)


def _active_lp_local(h, n, max_s):
    n = int(n)
    max_s = min(float(max_s), _tl())
    if max_s < 4.0:
        h = _proj_feasible(h, n)
        return h, _c5(h)
    h = _proj_feasible(h, n).copy()
    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
    cur = float(np.max(c))
    dx = 2.0 / n
    best = h.copy()
    bestc = cur * dx
    noimp = 0
    deadline = time.time() + max_s
    step = 0.04
    topk = 25
    while time.time() < deadline and _tl() > 2.0:
        ordered = np.argsort(c)[::-1]
        gap = max(0.3, 0.1 * cur)
        active = []
        for p in ordered:
            pp = int(p)
            if float(c[pp]) >= cur - gap:
                active.append(pp)
            if len(active) >= topk:
                break
        if len(active) < 2:
            for p in ordered[:3]:
                active.append(int(p))
        Glist = []
        nm1 = n - 1
        for p in active:
            gg = _grad_count(h, p - nm1)
            if float(np.linalg.norm(gg)) > 1e-10:
                Glist.append(gg)
        improved = False
        if Glist:
            G = np.vstack(Glist)
            lb = -np.minimum(h, step)
            ub = np.minimum(1.0 - h, step)
            mact = len(G)
            A = np.empty((mact, n + 1), dtype=np.float64)
            A[:, :n] = G
            A[:, n] = -1.0
            Aeq = np.zeros((1, n + 1), dtype=np.float64)
            Aeq[0, :n] = 1.0
            bounds = []
            for i in range(n):
                bounds.append((float(lb[i]), float(ub[i])))
            bounds.append((-1e9, 1e9))
            obj = np.zeros(n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
            obj[n] = 1.0
            sol = None
            try:
                sol = linprog(obj, A_ub=A, b_ub=np.zeros(mact), A_eq=Aeq, b_eq=[0.0],
                              bounds=bounds, method='highs',
                              options={'presolve': True})
            except Exception:
                try:
                    sol = linprog(obj, A_ub=A, b_ub=np.zeros(mact), A_eq=Aeq, b_eq=[0.0],
                                  bounds=bounds)
                except Exception:
                    sol = None
            if sol is not None and getattr(sol, 'success', False):
                d = np.asarray(sol.x[:n], dtype=np.float64)
                mu = float(sol.x[n])
                if mu < -1e-9:
                    if np.all(np.isfinite(d)) and float(np.max(np.abs(d))) > 1e-13:
                        h2, c2, v2, ok = _try_direction_full(h, n, c, cur, d)
                        if ok:
                            h = h2
                            c = c2
                            cur = v2
                            improved = True
        if not improved:
            h2, c2, v2, ok = _gradient_step_candidate(h, n, c, cur, step)
            if ok:
                h = h2
                c = c2
                cur = v2
                improved = True
        if improved:
            noimp = 0
            ev = cur * dx
            if ev < bestc - 1e-14:
                bestc = ev
                best = h.copy()
        else:
            noimp += 1
            if noimp > 40:
                break
    ev = _c5(h)
    if ev < bestc:
        best = h.copy()
        bestc = ev
    else:
        best = _proj_feasible(best, n)
        bestc = _c5(best)
    return best, bestc


def _fast_local_search(h, n, max_s, rng):
    n = int(n)
    max_s = min(float(max_s), _tl())
    if max_s < 2.0:
        h = _proj_feasible(h, n)
        return h, _c5(h)
    h = _proj_feasible(h, n).copy()
    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
    cur = float(np.max(c))
    dx = 2.0 / n
    best = h.copy()
    bestc = cur * dx
    info = _build_delta_info(n)
    g = None
    next_grad = 0
    it = 0
    noimp = 0
    alphas = (0.5, 0.25, 0.75, 0.1, 0.9, 0.35, 0.65, 0.15)
    recomp = 1000 if n <= 256 else 2500
    deadline = time.time() + max_s
    while time.time() < deadline and _tl() > 2.0:
        it += 1
        if it >= next_grad:
            g = _weighted_grad(h, c, 80.0)
            next_grad = it + 150
        r = rng.random_sample()
        accepted = False
        i = int(rng.randint(n))
        j = int(rng.randint(n))
        if i == j and n > 1:
            j = (j + 1) % n
        if r < 0.45:
            if i != j:
                a = float(h[i])
                b = float(h[j])
                if abs(a - b) > 1e-13:
                    ok, vm = _try_transfer_inplace(h, c, info, n, i, j, b - a, a - b, cur)
                    if ok:
                        accepted = True
                        cur = vm
        elif r < 0.85:
            if i != j:
                a = float(h[i])
                b = float(h[j])
                if rng.random_sample() < 0.5:
                    lim = min(1.0 - a, b) - 1e-12
                    if lim > 1e-13:
                        t = alphas[int(rng.randint(len(alphas)))] * lim
                        ok, vm = _try_transfer_inplace(h, c, info, n, i, j, t, -t, cur)
                        if ok:
                            accepted = True
                            cur = vm
                else:
                    lim = min(1.0 - b, a) - 1e-12
                    if lim > 1e-13:
                        t = -alphas[int(rng.randint(len(alphas)))] * lim
                        ok, vm = _try_transfer_inplace(h, c, info, n, i, j, t, -t, cur)
                        if ok:
                            accepted = True
                            cur = vm
        else:
            if g is None:
                g = _weighted_grad(h, c, 80.0)
            if n > 1 and float(np.max(g) - np.min(g)) > 1e-12:
                low = np.argsort(g)
                high = np.argsort(-g)
                klow = min(12, n)
                khigh = min(12, n)
                for _ in range(3):
                    ii = int(low[int(rng.randint(klow))])
                    jj = int(high[int(rng.randint(khigh))])
                    if ii == jj:
                        continue
                    a = float(h[ii])
                    b = float(h[jj])
                    if g[ii] < g[jj]:
                        lim = min(1.0 - a, b) - 1e-12
                        if lim > 1e-13:
                            t = alphas[int(rng.randint(len(alphas)))] * lim
                            ok, vm = _try_transfer_inplace(h, c, info, n, ii, jj, t, -t, cur)
                            if ok:
                                accepted = True
                                cur = vm
                                break
                    else:
                        lim = min(1.0 - b, a) - 1e-12
                        if lim > 1e-13:
                            t = -alphas[int(rng.randint(len(alphas)))] * lim
                            ok, vm = _try_transfer_inplace(h, c, info, n, ii, jj, t, -t, cur)
                            if ok:
                                accepted = True
                                cur = vm
                                break
        if accepted:
            noimp = 0
        else:
            noimp += 1
            if noimp > 30000:
                break
        if it % recomp == 0:
            c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
            cur = float(np.max(c))
            ev = cur * dx
            if ev < bestc - 1e-14:
                bestc = ev
                best = h.copy()
    ev = _c5(h)
    if ev < bestc:
        best = h.copy()
        bestc = ev
    else:
        best = _proj_feasible(best, n)
        bestc = _c5(best)
    return best, bestc


def _pair_sweep(h, n, max_s):
    n = int(n)
    max_s = min(float(max_s), _tl())
    if max_s < 1.5:
        h = _proj_feasible(h, n)
        return h, _c5(h)
    h = _proj_feasible(h, n).copy()
    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
    cur = float(np.max(c))
    dx = 2.0 / n
    best = h.copy()
    bestc = cur * dx
    info = _build_delta_info(n)
    noimp = 0
    pairs = 0
    recomp = 500 if n <= 220 else 1200
    deadline = time.time() + max_s
    for i in range(n):
        if time.time() >= deadline or _tl() <= 2.0:
            break
        hi = float(h[i])
        for j in range(i + 1, n):
            if time.time() >= deadline or _tl() <= 2.0:
                break
            pairs += 1
            a = hi
            b = float(h[j])
            accepted = False
            if abs(a - b) > 1e-13:
                ok, vm = _try_transfer_inplace(h, c, info, n, i, j, b - a, a - b, cur)
                if ok:
                    cur = vm
                    hi = float(h[i])
                    accepted = True
            if not accepted:
                lim = min(1.0 - a, b) - 1e-12
                if lim > 1e-13:
                    for al in (0.5, 0.2, 0.8):
                        t = al * lim
                        ok, vm = _try_transfer_inplace(h, c, info, n, i, j, t, -t, cur)
                        if ok:
                            cur = vm
                            hi = float(h[i])
                            accepted = True
                            break
            if not accepted:
                lim = min(1.0 - b, a) - 1e-12
                if lim > 1e-13:
                    for al in (0.5, 0.2, 0.8):
                        t = -al * lim
                        ok, vm = _try_transfer_inplace(h, c, info, n, i, j, t, -t, cur)
                        if ok:
                            cur = vm
                            hi = float(h[i])
                            accepted = True
                            break
            if accepted:
                noimp = 0
            else:
                noimp += 1
            if pairs % recomp == 0:
                c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
                cur = float(np.max(c))
                ev = cur * dx
                if ev < bestc - 1e-14:
                    bestc = ev
                    best = h.copy()
                hi = float(h[i])
                if noimp > 6000:
                    noimp = 0
    ev = _c5(h)
    if ev < bestc:
        best = h.copy()
        bestc = ev
    else:
        best = _proj_feasible(best, n)
        bestc = _c5(best)
    return best, bestc


def _quick_symmetries(h, n):
    n = int(n)
    h = _proj_feasible(h, n).copy()
    best = h.copy()
    bc = _c5(best)
    step_list = []
    if n <= 256:
        for s in range(n):
            step_list.append(s)
    else:
        used = set()
        for s in range(128):
            ss = (s * n) // 128
            if ss not in used:
                used.add(ss)
                step_list.append(ss)
    for s in step_list:
        h2 = np.roll(h, int(s))
        c2 = _c5(h2)
        if c2 < bc - 1e-14:
            best = h2.copy()
            bc = float(c2)
    hr = best[::-1].copy()
    c2 = _c5(hr)
    if c2 < bc:
        best = hr
        bc = float(c2)
    rev_steps = []
    for s in step_list:
        if int(s) != 0 and int(s) not in rev_steps:
            rev_steps.append(int(s))
    for s in rev_steps:
        h2 = np.roll(hr, s)
        c2 = _c5(h2)
        if c2 < bc:
            best = h2.copy()
            bc = float(c2)
    return _proj_feasible(best, n), float(bc)


def _gen_initial(n, rng):
    n = int(n)
    best = None
    bc = 1e100
    t = np.arange(n, dtype=float)
    periods = (3, 4, 5, 6, 7, 8, 10, 12)
    amps = (0.2, 0.35, 0.5, 0.7)
    phases = (0.0, 0.5, 1.0, 1.5, 2.0, 2.5)
    for period in periods:
        for amp in amps:
            for ph in phases:
                h = 0.5 + amp * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * t / float(period) + ph)
                h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
                h = _proj_feasible(h, n)
                c5 = _c5(h)
                if c5 < bc:
                    bc = float(c5)
                    best = h.copy()
    k = n // 2
    for _ in range(20):
        h = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        idx = rng.choice(n, k, replace=False)
        h[idx] = 1.0
        h = _proj_feasible(h, n)
        c5 = _c5(h)
        if c5 < bc:
            bc = float(c5)
            best = h.copy()
    h = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for i in range(n):
        y = i ^ (i >> 1)
        if bin(y).count("1") & 1:
            h[i] = 1.0
    h = _proj_feasible(h, n)
    c5 = _c5(h)
    if c5 < bc:
        bc = float(c5)
        best = h.copy()
    if best is None:
        best = np.full(n, 0.5, dtype=np.float64)
    return _proj_feasible(best, n)


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    global _T0, _TB
    _T0 = time.time()
    _TB = float(budget_s)
    rng = np.random.RandomState(int(seed))

    hpar = None
    try:
        hpar = np.array(initial_h_values, dtype=np.float64)
    except Exception:
        hpar = None

    if hpar is None or len(hpar) == 0:
        hpar = _gen_initial(160, rng)
        npar = int(len(hpar))
    else:
        hpar = np.asarray(hpar, dtype=np.float64)
        npar = int(len(hpar))
        if npar > 512:
            hpar = _interp_f(hpar, npar, 512)
            npar = 512

    hpar = _proj_feasible(hpar, npar)
    hbest, cbest = _quick_symmetries(hpar, npar)
    nbest = int(npar)
    hwork = hbest.copy()

    scl = max(0.2, min(1.0, budget_s / 1300.0))

    t1 = max(4.0, 65.0 * scl)
    hwork, cw = _fast_local_search(hwork, nbest, t1, rng)
    if cw < cbest:
        hbest = hwork.copy()
        cbest = float(cw)
    else:
        hwork = hbest.copy()

    t2 = max(8.0, 28.0 * scl)
    hwork = _smooth_slsqp(hwork, nbest, 200.0, t2)
    cw = _c5(hwork)
    if cw < cbest:
        hbest = hwork.copy()
        cbest = float(cw)
    elif cw > cbest + 1e-6:
        hwork = hbest.copy()

    t3 = max(4.0, 55.0 * scl)
    hwork, cw = _pair_sweep(hwork, nbest, t3)
    if cw < cbest:
        hbest = hwork.copy()
        cbest = float(cw)
    else:
        hwork = hbest.copy()

    t4 = max(4.0, 55.0 * scl)
    hwork, cw = _fast_local_search(hwork, nbest, t4, rng)
    if cw < cbest:
        hbest = hwork.copy()
        cbest = float(cw)
    else:
        hwork = hbest.copy()

    t5 = max(3.0, 30.0 * scl)
    hwork, cw = _active_lp_local(hwork, nbest, t5)
    if cw < cbest:
        hbest = hwork.copy()
        cbest = float(cw)
    else:
        hwork = hbest.copy()

    t6 = max(4.0, 55.0 * scl)
    hwork, cw = _pair_sweep(hwork, nbest, t6)
    if cw < cbest:
        hbest = hwork.copy()
        cbest = float(cw)
    else:
        hwork = hbest.copy()

    t7 = max(8.0, 30.0 * scl)
    hwork = _smooth_slsqp(hwork, nbest, 1000.0, t7)
    cw = _c5(hwork)
    if cw < cbest:
        hbest = hwork.copy()
        cbest = float(cw)
    elif cw > cbest + 1e-6:
        hwork = hbest.copy()

    t8 = max(4.0, 45.0 * scl)
    hwork, cw = _fast_local_search(hwork, nbest, t8, rng)
    if cw < cbest:
        hbest = hwork.copy()
        cbest = float(cw)
    else:
        hwork = hbest.copy()

    t9 = max(3.0, 20.0 * scl)
    hwork, cw = _active_lp_local(hwork, nbest, t9)
    if cw < cbest:
        hbest = hwork.copy()
        cbest = float(cw)
    else:
        hwork = hbest.copy()

    t10 = max(3.0, 40.0 * scl)
    hwork, cw = _pair_sweep(hwork, nbest, t10)
    if cw < cbest:
        hbest = hwork.copy()
        cbest = float(cw)
    else:
        hwork = hbest.copy()

    t11 = max(8.0, 32.0 * scl)
    hwork = _smooth_slsqp(hwork, nbest, 3000.0, t11)
    cw = _c5(hwork)
    if cw < cbest:
        hbest = hwork.copy()
        cbest = float(cw)
    elif cw > cbest + 1e-6:
        hwork = hbest.copy()

    t12 = max(3.0, 35.0 * scl)
    hwork, cw = _fast_local_search(hwork, nbest, t12, rng)
    if cw < cbest:
        hbest = hwork.copy()
        cbest = float(cw)

    sigmas = (0.02, 0.05, 0.10)
    ptry = 0
    while _tl() > 260.0 and ptry < len(sigmas):
        sigma = float(sigmas[ptry])
        ptry += 1
        noise = rng.randn(nbest)
        noise -= float(np.mean(noise))
        h2 = _proj_feasible(hbest + sigma * noise, nbest)
        h2, c2 = _quick_symmetries(h2, nbest)
        if c2 < cbest:
            hbest = h2.copy()
            cbest = float(c2)

        h2, c2 = _pair_sweep(h2, nbest, min(45.0 * scl, _tl()))
        if c2 < cbest:
            hbest = h2.copy()
            cbest = float(c2)

        h2, c2 = _fast_local_search(h2, nbest, min(30.0 * scl, _tl()), rng)
        if c2 < cbest:
            hbest = h2.copy()
            cbest = float(c2)

        if _tl() > 45.0:
            h2 = _smooth_slsqp(h2, nbest, 800.0, min(22.0 * scl, _tl()))
            c2 = _c5(h2)
            if c2 < cbest:
                hbest = h2.copy()
                cbest = float(c2)

        h2, c2 = _pair_sweep(h2, nbest, min(20.0 * scl, _tl()))
        if c2 < cbest:
            hbest = h2.copy()
            cbest = float(c2)

    nn_list = []
    base = int(nbest)
    for nn in (base + 32, base + 64, base - 32, base + 16, base - 16, base + 80, base + 128):
        if nn % 2 != 0:
            nn += 1
        if 64 <= nn <= 500 and nn not in nn_list:
            nn_list.append(int(nn))

    for nn in nn_list:
        if _tl() < 170.0:
            break
        h2 = _interp_f(hbest, nbest, nn)
        h2 = _proj_feasible(h2, nn)
        h2, c2 = _quick_symmetries(h2, nn)
        if c2 < cbest:
            hbest = h2.copy()
            cbest = float(c2)
            nbest = int(nn)

        h2, c2 = _fast_local_search(h2, nn, min(55.0 * scl, _tl()), rng)
        if c2 < cbest:
            hbest = h2.copy()
            cbest = float(c2)
            nbest = int(nn)

        h2, c2 = _pair_sweep(h2, nn, min(30.0 * scl, _tl()))
        if c2 < cbest:
            hbest = h2.copy()
            cbest = float(c2)
            nbest = int(nn)

        if nbest <= 320 and _tl() > 80.0:
            h2 = _smooth_slsqp(h2, nbest, 800.0, min(20.0 * scl, _tl()))
            c2 = _c5(h2)
            if c2 < cbest:
                hbest = h2.copy()
                cbest = float(c2)
                nbest = int(np.asarray(hbest).shape[0])

        if _tl() > 45.0:
            h2, c2 = _fast_local_search(h2, nbest, min(18.0 * scl, _tl()), rng)
            if c2 < cbest:
                hbest = h2.copy()
                cbest = float(c2)
                nbest = int(np.asarray(hbest).shape[0])

    if _tl() > 15.0:
        hbest, cbest = _pair_sweep(hbest, nbest, min(40.0 * scl, _tl()))

    if _tl() > 25.0:
        hnew = _smooth_slsqp(hbest, nbest, 5000.0, min(28.0 * scl, _tl()))
        cnew = _c5(hnew)
        if cnew < cbest:
            hbest = hnew.copy()
            cbest = float(cnew)
            nbest = int(np.asarray(hbest).shape[0])

    if _tl() > 15.0:
        hnew, cnew = _fast_local_search(hbest, nbest, min(28.0 * scl, _tl()), rng)
        if cnew < cbest:
            hbest = hnew.copy()
            cbest = float(cnew)
            nbest = int(np.asarray(hbest).shape[0])

    hbest = _proj_feasible(hbest, nbest)
    cbest = _c5(hbest)
    try:
        ec = evaluate_erdos_solution(hbest)
        ec = float(ec)
        if np.isfinite(ec) and ec < cbest:
            cbest = ec
    except Exception:
        pass

    return (np.asarray(hbest, dtype=np.float64), float(cbest), int(nbest))
```
